## Comprehensive Checkpoint & Transcript Flow Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the external-agents codebase, here's the complete flow of how checkpoints are created, transcripts are captured, and how they're associated together in the kiro agent implementation:

### 1. HOOK LIFECYCLE & CHECKPOINT CREATION

**Hook Installation** (`hooks.go`):
- Kiro registers 5 hooks during `entire enable`:
  - `agent-spawn` (Type 1) — When agent starts
  - `user-prompt-submit` (Type 2) — When user submits a prompt
  - `pre-tool-use` (Type 3) — Before tool execution
  - `post-tool-use` (Type 4) — After tool execution
  - `stop` (Type 3) — When agent stops/session ends
- Hooks are installed in two locations:
  - CLI hooks: `.kiro/agents/entire.json`
  - IDE hooks: `.kiro/hooks/*.kiro.hook` files
  - VS Code settings: `.vscode/settings.json` (trusted commands)

**Hook Parsing Flow** (`ParseHook()` in hooks.go):

```
agent-spawn hook:
├─ Generate new sessionID (random hex)
├─ Cache it locally: .entire/tmp/kiro-active-session
└─ Return EventJSON (Type 1) with sessionID

user-prompt-submit hook:
├─ Read cached sessionID (or generate new one)
├─ Extract prompt from hook input
└─ Return EventJSON (Type 2) with prompt + sessionID

stop hook (CRITICAL):
├─ Read cached sessionID
├─ If missing, query kiro-cli database for most recent conversation
├─ Fallback: generate new sessionID
├─ Capture transcript via captureTranscriptForStop()
├─ Clear cached sessionID
└─ Return EventJSON (Type 3) with sessionID + sessionRef (transcript path)
```

### 2. TRANSCRIPT CAPTURE & CACHING

**Session State Flow** (`agent.go`):
- Session lifecycle: `ReadSession()` → `WriteSession()`
- Sessions stored in: `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`
- Each session file contains:
  - `session_id`: Unique identifier
  - `agent_name`: "kiro"
  - `repo_path`: Repository root
  - `session_ref`: Path to transcript file (e.g., `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`)
  - `start_time`: RFC3339 timestamp
  - `native_data`: Raw transcript bytes
  - `modified_files`: List of files touched

**Transcript Capture Sources** (`transcript.go`):
The `captureTranscriptForStop()` method tries three sources in order:

1. **CLI Transcript** (`ensureCachedTranscript()`):
   - Queries kiro-cli SQLite database: `~/Library/Application Support/kiro-cli/data.sqlite3` (macOS)
   - Query: `SELECT value FROM conversations_v2 WHERE key='<cwd>' ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1`
   - Extracts `conversation_id` from JSON response
   - Caches to: `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`

2. **IDE Transcript** (`ensureIDETranscript()`):
   - Reads IDE workspace sessions from: `~/Library/Application Support/Kiro/User/globalStorage/kiro.kiroagent/workspace-sessions/<base64(cwd)>/`
   - Parses `sessions.json` index
   - Loads latest session file: `<sessionID>.json`
   - Converts IDE format to CLI format via `convertIDETranscript()`
   - Caches to: `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`

3. **Placeholder** (`createPlaceholderTranscript()`):
   - Fallback: creates empty JSON object `{}`
   - Ensures transcript always exists even if capture fails

**Transcript Format Handling** (`parseTranscript()`):

```go
CLI Format (kiroTranscript):
├─ conversation_id: string
└─ history: []kiroHistoryEntry
    └─ []{
        user: {
          content: json.RawMessage ({"Prompt": {"prompt": "..."}})
          timestamp: string (optional)
        }
        assistant: json.RawMessage ({"ToolUse": {...}} or {"Response": {...}})
      }

IDE Format (kiroIDETranscript) [auto-converted]:
└─ history: []kiroIDEHistoryEntry
    └─ []{
        message: {
          role: "user" | "assistant"
          content: json.RawMessage (varies)
        }
      }
```

### 3. CHECKPOINT ASSOCIATION

**Checkpoint Metadata Structure** (from test utilities):
```
entire/checkpoints/v1/
├─ <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/          # Checkpoint directory (2-level hash)
│  ├─ metadata.json             # Top-level checkpoint metadata
│  └─ <sessionIndex>/           # Per-session directory
│     ├─ metadata.json          # Session metadata (checkpoint_id, created_at, agent, files_touched)
│     ├─ full.jsonl             # Session transcript (JSONL format)
│     ├─ content_hash.txt       # SHA256 hash of transcript
│     └─ prompt.txt             # User prompts
```

**SessionRef Structure** (`metadata.go`):
Each checkpoint metadata contains:
```go
Sessions: []SessionRef{
  {
    Metadata: "path/to/session/metadata.json"
    Transcript: "path/to/session/full.jsonl"
    Context: "context path"
    ContentHash: "content_hash.txt"
    Prompt: "prompt.txt"
  }
}
```

### 4. TRANSCRIPT ANALYSIS (Checkpoint Integration)

The Agent implements `transcriptAnalyzer` interface to extract data from saved transcripts:

**Key Methods**:
- `GetTranscriptPosition(path)` — Returns count of history entries (offset)
- `ExtractModifiedFiles(path, offset)` — Extracts file paths from assistant ToolUse calls
  - Looks for: `fs_write`, `fs_edit` tool uses
  - Returns: list of modified files + current position
- `ExtractPrompts(path, offset)` — Extracts user prompts from history
- `ExtractSummary(path)` — Gets last assistant response

**Modified Files Extraction** (`extractModifiedFilesFromHistory()`):
```
For each history entry:
  └─ For each Assistant response:
      └─ If ToolUse content:
          └─ For each tool call where name in {fs_write, fs_edit}:
              └─ Extract path field from args
                  (tries: "path", "file_path", "filename")
```

### 5. PROTOCOL FLOW

**Main Agent Commands** (`main.go` subcommands):

```
Agent Info Flow:
  info → InfoResponse{
    protocol_version: 1
    name: "kiro"
    hook_names: [agent-spawn, user-prompt-submit, pre-tool-use, post-tool-use, stop]
    capabilities: {hooks: true, transcript_analyzer: true, uses_terminal: true}
    protected_dirs: [".kiro"]
  }

Session Flow:
  read-session → ReadSession() → AgentSessionJSON{
    session_id, session_ref, native_data (transcript bytes)
  }
  write-session → WriteSession() → writes to session_ref path

Transcript Flow:
  read-transcript → ReadTranscript(session_ref) → []byte
  chunk-transcript → ChunkTranscript(content, maxSize) → [][]byte
  reassemble-transcript → ReassembleTranscript(chunks) → []byte

Analysis Flow:
  get-transcript-position → position: int
  extract-modified-files → {files: []string, current_position: int}
  extract-prompts → {prompts: []string}
  extract-summary → {summary: string, has_summary: bool}
```

### 6. KEY FILES AND PATHS

**Kiro Agent Implementation**:
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/internal/kiro/agent.go` — Main Agent struct and session management
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/internal/kiro/transcript.go` — Transcript capture, parsing, and analysis
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/internal/kiro/hooks.go` — Hook installation and parsing
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/internal/kiro/types.go` — Data structures
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/cmd/entire-agent-kiro/main.go` — CLI entry point

**Protocol Handlers**:
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/internal/protocol/protocol.go` — Handler implementations
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/internal/protocol/types.go` — JSON structures

**Test Utilities**:
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/e2e/testutil/metadata.go` — Checkpoint/session metadata structs
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/e2e/testutil/assertions.go` — Checkpoint validation helpers
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/e2e/testutil/artifacts.go` — Artifact capture (includes checkpoint metadata)
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/e2e/testutil/repo.go` — RepoState, session management, checkpoint reading

**Lifecycle Tests**:
- `/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/e2e/lifecycle_test.go` — End-to-end flow tests

### 7. FULL CHECKPOINT CREATION SEQUENCE

```
1. User runs prompt via kiro-cli (agent start)
   └─ Hooks fire: agent-spawn
      ├─ Generate sessionID
      └─ Cache: .entire/tmp/kiro-active-session

2. User submits prompt
   └─ Hook fires: user-prompt-submit
      └─ eventJSON(Type 2, sessionID, prompt)

3. Agent processes prompt (tool uses occur)
   └─ Hooks fire: pre-tool-use, post-tool-use
      └─ Currently no-op in kiro

4. User commits changes to git
   └─ Entire CLI triggers checkpoint creation
      ├─ Reads session data (ReadSession):
      │  └─ sessionID from hook
      │  └─ sessionRef from captureTranscriptForStop()
      ├─ Captures transcript from:
      │  1. Kiro CLI database (SQLite)
      │  2. IDE workspace sessions
      │  3. Placeholder JSON
      ├─ Writes session to .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json
      └─ Returns AgentSessionJSON with native_data = transcript bytes

5. Checkpoint commit created on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   ├─ Session metadata written: <cpID>/<idx>/metadata.json
   │  ├─ checkpoint_id: matches commit trailer
   │  ├─ session_id: from hook
   │  ├─ created_at: timestamp
   │  ├─ files_touched: extracted via ExtractModifiedFiles()
   │  ├─ transcript_path: points to full.jsonl
   │  └─ agent: "kiro"
   ├─ Transcript written: <cpID>/<idx>/full.jsonl
   │  └─ Raw transcript content (JSONL)
   ├─ Content hash: <cpID>/<idx>/content_hash.txt
   │  └─ SHA256(transcript)
   └─ Prompt written: <cpID>/<idx>/prompt.txt
      └─ Extracted via ExtractPrompts()

6. User code commit receives Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   └─ Format: Entire-Checkpoint: <12-char hex ID>
```

### 8. CRITICAL CONNECTIONS

**SessionID Generation** (`generateSessionID()`):
- 16 random bytes → hex-encoded string
- Fallback: `kiro-<nanoseconds>` if rand fails

**Transcript Path Resolution** (`ResolveSessionFile()`):
- Pattern: `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`
- Used as reference (session_ref) in checkpoints

**Hook Command Format**:
```bash
# Production
entire hooks kiro <hook-name>

# Local dev  
go run ${KIRO_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks kiro <hook-name>
```

**Database Query for Transcript**:
```sql
SELECT value FROM conversations_v2 
WHERE key = '<cwd>' 
ORDER BY updated_at DESC 
LIMIT 1
```
The `value` field contains full JSON conversation data

---

This is a comprehensive view of how checkpoints and transcripts flow through the system, from hook invocation through checkpoint creation and checkpoint branch commits.

## Summary of Checkpoint Structure Analysis

I've thoroughly explored the checkpoint structure in the test kiro repo and found **critical evidence of a transcript mismatch bug**. Here are my findings:

### 1. **Checkpoints Exist and Structure**

The repo contains checkpoints stored in:
- **Location**: `/Users/alisha/Projects/test-repos/kirocli/.git/entire-sessions/` (session JSON files)
- **Git tree storage**: Checkpoint data is stored as git objects in the tree structure under directories like `2c/357c43b5d8/` and `be/9ac770be8f/`
- **Checkpoint refs**: Stored in `.git/refs/heads/entire/checkpoints/v1` and `.git/refs/remotes/origin/entire/checkpoints/v1`

### 2. **Three Main Checkpoints Found**

1. **Checkpoint `27bd9011ef0e`** (oldest)
   - Prompt: "Add hello world"
   - Stored transcript size: Not queried

2. **Checkpoint `be9ac770be8f`** (middle)
   - Session: `b5cab790dca2bb36ee4a5fb0bdd17335`
   - Prompt: "create a program that plays rock paper scissors and prints out winner"
   - Transcript size: 23,280 bytes
   - Content hash: `sha256:c23a677c8bac4719e745b7bf3a13754f0f9c2d30712594dd0e3f9e851091325f`

3. **Checkpoint `2c357c43b5d8`** (latest - appears in 3 commits)
   - Primary session: `98d453b97ccfc743140f288130ce0698`
   - Prompt: "yes golang"
   - Transcript size: 92,300 bytes
   - Content hash: `sha256:749a663723d9b1de7a9c3fe71544e8961a921de8fe0a81b26300f10a4530d0ae`

### 3. **EVIDENCE OF TRANSCRIPT MISMATCH BUG - CONFIRMED**

**The bug exists in checkpoint `2c357c43b5d8` session 2:**

- **Stored prompt.txt**: `yes golang`
- **Actual transcript content (first prompt in full.jsonl)**: `create a program that plays rock paper scissors and prints out winner`
- **The transcript is from the PREVIOUS checkpoint** (`be9ac770be8f`)

**Specific evidence:**
- File: `/Users/alisha/Projects/test-repos/kirocli/.git/entire-sessions/2c/357c43b5d8/2/prompt.txt` = "yes golang"
- File: `/Users/alisha/Projects/test-repos/kirocli/.git/entire-sessions/2c/357c43b5d8/2/full.jsonl` starts with "create a program that plays rock paper scissors and prints out winner"
- This exact text is the prompt.txt from `be/9ac770be8f/2/prompt.txt`
- The transcript size (92,300 bytes) does NOT match the `be9ac770be8f/2` transcript (23,280 bytes), indicating this is a concatenation or different content

### 4. **Progression of Latest Checkpoint Commits**

Three git commits all reference checkpoint `2c357c43b5d8`:
1. **077952c** - Only has session 0 (initial metadata)
2. **afb5985** - Has sessions 0 and 1 (no full.jsonl, just metadata)
3. **afc5e80** - Has sessions 0, 1, and 2 (session 2 has the mismatched transcript)

### 5. **Session Metadata Files**

Session metadata is stored in `.git/entire-sessions/` with IDs:
- `98d453b97ccfc743140f288130ce0698.json` - Last prompt: "yes golang", checkpoint_transcript_size: 92300
- `52db0373ed04eaf5a689e974519e0bea.json` - Last prompt: "entire status"
- `b5cab790dca2bb36ee4a5fb0bdd17335.json` - Last prompt: "rock paper scissors", checkpoint_transcript_size: 23280

The issue is that session `98d453b97ccfc743140f288130ce0698` (which has "yes golang" as the prompt) has its stored full.jsonl transcript containing the content from the previous session's checkpoint.

```
     1→package kiro
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"encoding/base64"

     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"os"

    17→)
    18→
    19→var runSQLiteCommand = func(args ...string) ([]byte, error) {
    20→	return exec.Command("sqlite3", args...).Output()
    21→}
    22→
    23→var kiroFileModificationTools = map[string]struct{}{
    24→	"fs_write": {},
    25→	"fs_edit":  {},
    26→}
    27→
    28→type ideSessionIndexEntry struct {
    29→	SessionID   string `json:"sessionId"`
    30→	DateCreated string `json:"dateCreated"`
    31→}
    32→
    33→func (a *Agent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    34→	return os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
    35→}
    36→
    37→func (a *Agent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    38→	if maxSize <= 0 {
    39→		return nil, errors.New("max-size must be greater than zero")
    40→	}
    41→	if len(content) == 0 {
    42→		return [][]byte{[]byte{}}, nil
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	var chunks [][]byte
    46→	for start := 0; start < len(content); start += maxSize {
    47→		end := start + maxSize
    48→		if end > len(content) {
    49→			end = len(content)
    50→		}
    51→		chunk := make([]byte, end-start)
    52→		copy(chunk, content[start:end])
    53→		chunks = append(chunks, chunk)
    54→	}
    55→	return chunks, nil
    56→}
    57→
    58→func (a *Agent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
    59→	return bytes.Join(chunks, nil), nil
    60→}
    61→
    62→func (a *Agent) querySessionID(cwd string) (string, error) {
    63→	db, err := kiroCLIDataDBPath()
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		return "", err
    66→	}
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(db); err != nil {
    68→		return "", fmt.Errorf("kiro database not found at %s: %w", db, err)
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	query := fmt.Sprintf(
    72→		"SELECT json_extract(value, '$.conversation_id') FROM conversations_v2 WHERE key = '%s' ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1",
    73→		escapeSQLString(cwd),
    74→	)
    75→
    76→	out, err := runSQLiteCommand("-json", db, query)
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		return "", fmt.Errorf("sqlite3 query failed: %w", err)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	result := strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
    82→	if result == "" || result == "[]" {
    83→		return "", nil
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	var rows []map[string]string
    87→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(result), &rows); err != nil {
    88→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse sqlite3 output: %w", err)
    89→	}
    90→	if len(rows) == 0 {
    91→		return "", nil
    92→	}
    93→	for _, value := range rows[0] {
    94→		return value, nil
    95→	}
    96→	return "", nil
    97→}
    98→
    99→func (a *Agent) ensureCachedTranscript(cwd string, sessionID string) (string, error) {
   100→	db, err := kiroCLIDataDBPath()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return "", err
   103→	}
   104→	if _, err := os.Stat(db); err != nil {
   105→		return "", fmt.Errorf("kiro database not found at %s: %w", db, err)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	query := fmt.Sprintf(
   109→		"SELECT value FROM conversations_v2 WHERE key = '%s' ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1",
   110→		escapeSQLString(cwd),
   111→	)
   112→
   113→	out, err := runSQLiteCommand(db, query)
   114→	if err != nil {
   115→		return "", fmt.Errorf("sqlite3 transcript query failed: %w", err)
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	transcript := strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   119→	if transcript == "" {
   120→		return "", errors.New("no transcript found")
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	cachePath, err := a.cacheTranscriptPath(cwd, sessionID)
   124→	if err != nil {
   125→		return "", err
   126→	}
   127→	if err := os.WriteFile(cachePath, []byte(transcript), 0o600); err != nil {
   128→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write cached transcript: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→	return cachePath, nil
   131→}
   132→
   133→func (a *Agent) ensureIDETranscript(cwd string, sessionID string) (string, error) {
   134→	sessionsDir, err := ideWorkspaceSessionsDir(cwd)
   135→	if err != nil {
   136→		return "", err
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	indexPath := filepath.Join(sessionsDir, "sessions.json")
   140→	indexData, err := os.ReadFile(indexPath)
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		return "", fmt.Errorf("IDE sessions.json not found: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	var sessions []ideSessionIndexEntry
   146→	if err := json.Unmarshal(indexData, &sessions); err != nil {
   147→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse IDE sessions.json: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→	if len(sessions) == 0 {
   150→		return "", errors.New("no IDE sessions found")
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	sort.Slice(sessions, func(i, j int) bool {
   154→		return sessions[i].DateCreated > sessions[j].DateCreated
   155→	})
   156→
   157→	ideSessionID := sessions[0].SessionID
   158→	if !filepath.IsLocal(ideSessionID + ".json") {
   159→		return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %q", ideSessionID)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(sessionsDir, ideSessionID+".json")
   163→	transcriptData, err := os.ReadFile(transcriptPath)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read IDE transcript %s: %w", transcriptPath, err)
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	cachePath, err := a.cacheTranscriptPath(cwd, sessionID)
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return "", err
   171→	}
   172→	if err := os.WriteFile(cachePath, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   173→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write cached IDE transcript: %w", err)
   174→	}
   175→	return cachePath, nil
   176→}
   177→
   178→func (a *Agent) captureTranscriptForStop(cwd string, sessionID string) string {
   179→	if sessionRef, err := a.ensureCachedTranscript(cwd, sessionID); err == nil && sessionRef != "" {
   180→		return sessionRef
   181→	}
   182→	if sessionRef, err := a.ensureIDETranscript(cwd, sessionID); err == nil && sessionRef != "" {
   183→		return sessionRef
   184→	}
   185→	return a.createPlaceholderTranscript(cwd, sessionID)
   186→}
   187→
   188→func (a *Agent) createPlaceholderTranscript(cwd string, sessionID string) string {
   189→	cachePath, err := a.cacheTranscriptPath(cwd, sessionID)
   190→	if err != nil {
   191→		return ""
   192→	}
   193→	if err := os.WriteFile(cachePath, []byte("{}"), 0o600); err != nil {
   194→		return ""
   195→	}
   196→	return cachePath
   197→}
   198→
   199→func (a *Agent) cacheTranscriptPath(cwd string, sessionID string) (string, error) {
   200→	repoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()
   201→	if repoRoot == "" {
   202→		repoRoot = cwd
   203→	}
   204→	sessionDir, err := a.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		return "", err
   207→	}
   208→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDir, 0o700); err != nil {
   209→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create cache dir: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→	return a.ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, sessionID), nil
   212→}
   213→
   214→func kiroDataDir() (string, error) {
   215→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   216→	if err != nil {
   217→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   218→	}
   219→	switch runtime.GOOS {
   220→	case "darwin":
   221→		return filepath.Join(home, "Library", "Application Support"), nil
   222→	default:
   223→		return filepath.Join(home, ".local", "share"), nil
   224→	}
   225→}
   226→
   227→func kiroCLIDataDBPath() (string, error) {
   228→	dataDir, err := kiroDataDir()
   229→	if err != nil {
   230→		return "", err
   231→	}
   232→	return filepath.Join(dataDir, "kiro-cli", "data.sqlite3"), nil
   233→}
   234→
   235→func ideWorkspaceSessionsDir(cwd string) (string, error) {
   236→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   237→	if err != nil {
   238→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	encoded := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(cwd))
   242→	switch runtime.GOOS {
   243→	case "darwin":
   244→		return filepath.Join(home, "Library", "Application Support", "Kiro", "User", "globalStorage", "kiro.kiroagent", "workspace-sessions", encoded), nil
   245→	default:
   246→		return filepath.Join(home, ".config", "Kiro", "User", "globalStorage", "kiro.kiroagent", "workspace-sessions", encoded), nil
   247→	}
   248→}
   249→
   250→func escapeSQLString(s string) string {
   251→	return strings.ReplaceAll(s, "'", "''")
   252→}
   253→
   254→func (a *Agent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   255→	transcript, err := readAndParseTranscript(path)
   256→	if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
   257→		return 0, nil
   258→	}
   259→	if err != nil {
   260→		return 0, err
   261→	}
   262→	return len(transcript.History), nil
   263→}
   264→
   265→func (a *Agent) ExtractModifiedFiles(path string, offset int) ([]string, int, error) {
   266→	transcript, err := readAndParseTranscript(path)
   267→	if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
   268→		return nil, 0, nil
   269→	}
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		return nil, 0, err
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	totalEntries := len(transcript.History)
   275→	if offset >= totalEntries {
   276→		return nil, totalEntries, nil
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	return extractModifiedFilesFromHistory(transcript.History[offset:]), totalEntries, nil
   280→}
   281→
   282→func (a *Agent) ExtractPrompts(path string, offset int) ([]string, error) {
   283→	transcript, err := readAndParseTranscript(path)
   284→	if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
   285→		return nil, nil
   286→	}
   287→	if err != nil {
   288→		return nil, err
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	var prompts []string
   292→	for i := offset; i < len(transcript.History); i++ {
   293→		if prompt := extractUserPrompt(transcript.History[i].User.Content); prompt != "" {
   294→			prompts = append(prompts, prompt)
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→	return prompts, nil
   298→}
   299→
   300→func (a *Agent) ExtractSummary(path string) (string, bool, error) {
   301→	transcript, err := readAndParseTranscript(path)
   302→	if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
   303→		return "", false, nil
   304→	}
   305→	if err != nil {
   306→		return "", false, err
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	summary := extractLastAssistantResponse(transcript.History)
   310→	return summary, summary != "", nil
   311→}
   312→
   313→func readAndParseTranscript(path string) (*kiroTranscript, error) {
   314→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
   315→	if err != nil {
   316→		return nil, err
   317→	}
   318→	return parseTranscript(data)
   319→}
   320→
   321→func parseTranscript(data []byte) (*kiroTranscript, error) {
   322→	if len(data) == 0 {
   323→		return &kiroTranscript{}, nil
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	var transcript kiroTranscript
   327→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &transcript); err != nil {
   328→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse kiro transcript: %w", err)
   329→	}
   330→	if isCLITranscript(&transcript) {
   331→		return &transcript, nil
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	if converted := tryParseIDETranscript(data); converted != nil {
   335→		return converted, nil
   336→	}
   337→
   338→	return &transcript, nil
   339→}
   340→
   341→func isCLITranscript(transcript *kiroTranscript) bool {
   342→	if len(transcript.History) == 0 {
   343→		return false
   344→	}
   345→	return len(transcript.History[0].User.Content) > 0 || len(transcript.History[0].Assistant) > 0
   346→}
   347→
   348→func tryParseIDETranscript(data []byte) *kiroTranscript {
   349→	var ide kiroIDETranscript
   350→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &ide); err != nil {
   351→		return nil
   352→	}
   353→	if len(ide.History) == 0 || ide.History[0].Message.Role == "" {
   354→		return nil
   355→	}
   356→	return convertIDETranscript(&ide)
   357→}
   358→
   359→func convertIDETranscript(ide *kiroIDETranscript) *kiroTranscript {
   360→	transcript := &kiroTranscript{}
   361→
   362→	var pendingUser *kiroIDEHistoryEntry
   363→	for i := range ide.History {
   364→		entry := &ide.History[i]
   365→		switch entry.Message.Role {
   366→		case "user":
   367→			if pendingUser != nil {
   368→				transcript.History = append(transcript.History, ideEntryToPaired(pendingUser, nil))
   369→			}
   370→			pendingUser = entry
   371→		case "assistant":
   372→			transcript.History = append(transcript.History, ideEntryToPaired(pendingUser, entry))
   373→			pendingUser = nil
   374→		}
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	if pendingUser != nil {
   378→		transcript.History = append(transcript.History, ideEntryToPaired(pendingUser, nil))
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	return transcript
   382→}
   383→
   384→func ideEntryToPaired(user, assistant *kiroIDEHistoryEntry) kiroHistoryEntry {
   385→	entry := kiroHistoryEntry{}
   386→
   387→	if user != nil {
   388→		prompt := extractIDEText(user.Message.Content, true)
   389→		if prompt != "" {
   390→			content, err := json.Marshal(kiroPromptContent{
   391→				Prompt: struct {
   392→					Prompt string `json:"prompt"`
   393→				}{Prompt: prompt},
   394→			})
   395→			if err == nil {
   396→				entry.User.Content = content
   397→			}
   398→		} else {
   399→			entry.User.Content = user.Message.Content
   400→		}
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	if assistant != nil {
   404→		text := extractIDEText(assistant.Message.Content, false)
   405→		if text != "" {
   406→			content, err := json.Marshal(kiroResponseContent{
   407→				Response: kiroResponsePayload{Content: text},
   408→			})
   409→			if err == nil {
   410→				entry.Assistant = content
   411→			}
   412→		} else {
   413→			entry.Assistant = assistant.Message.Content
   414→		}
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	return entry
   418→}
   419→
   420→// extractIDEText extracts text from a json.RawMessage that may be either a
   421→// plain string or an array of content blocks. When blocksFirst is true, it
   422→// tries to parse as blocks before falling back to a plain string (user
   423→// messages); otherwise it tries plain string first (assistant messages).
   424→func extractIDEText(content json.RawMessage, blocksFirst bool) string {
   425→	if len(content) == 0 {
   426→		return ""
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	tryBlocks := func() string {
   430→		var blocks []kiroIDEContentBlock
   431→		if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &blocks); err == nil && len(blocks) > 0 {
   432→			for _, block := range blocks {
   433→				if block.Type == "text" && block.Text != "" {
   434→					return block.Text
   435→				}
   436→			}
   437→		}
   438→		return ""
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	tryString := func() string {
   442→		var text string
   443→		if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &text); err == nil {
   444→			return text
   445→		}
   446→		return ""
   447→	}
   448→
   449→	if blocksFirst {
   450→		if s := tryBlocks(); s != "" {
   451→			return s
   452→		}
   453→		return tryString()
   454→	}
   455→	if s := tryString(); s != "" {
   456→		return s
   457→	}
   458→	return tryBlocks()
   459→}
   460→
   461→func extractUserPrompt(content json.RawMessage) string {
   462→	if len(content) == 0 {
   463→		return ""
   464→	}
   465→
   466→	var promptContent kiroPromptContent
   467→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &promptContent); err == nil && promptContent.Prompt.Prompt != "" {
   468→		return promptContent.Prompt.Prompt
   469→	}
   470→	return ""
   471→}
   472→
   473→func extractModifiedFilesFromHistory(entries []kiroHistoryEntry) []string {
   474→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   475→	var files []string
   476→
   477→	for i := range entries {
   478→		for _, path := range extractFilesFromAssistant(entries[i].Assistant) {
   479→			if path == "" || seen[path] {
   480→				continue
   481→			}
   482→			seen[path] = true
   483→			files = append(files, path)
   484→		}
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	return files
   488→}
   489→
   490→func extractFilesFromAssistant(raw json.RawMessage) []string {
   491→	if len(raw) == 0 {
   492→		return nil
   493→	}
   494→
   495→	var toolUseContent kiroToolUseContent
   496→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &toolUseContent); err != nil || len(toolUseContent.ToolUse.ToolUses) == 0 {
   497→		return nil
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	var paths []string
   501→	for _, call := range toolUseContent.ToolUse.ToolUses {
   502→		if !isFileModificationTool(call.Name) {
   503→			continue
   504→		}
   505→		if path := extractFilePath(call.Args); path != "" {
   506→			paths = append(paths, path)
   507→		}
   508→	}
   509→	return paths
   510→}
   511→
   512→func isFileModificationTool(name string) bool {
   513→	_, ok := kiroFileModificationTools[name]
   514→	return ok
   515→}
   516→
   517→func extractFilePath(args json.RawMessage) string {
   518→	if len(args) == 0 {
   519→		return ""
   520→	}
   521→
   522→	var fields map[string]json.RawMessage
   523→	if err := json.Unmarshal(args, &fields); err != nil {
   524→		return ""
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	for _, key := range []string{"path", "file_path", "filename"} {
   528→		raw, ok := fields[key]
   529→		if !ok {
   530→			continue
   531→		}
   532→		var path string
   533→		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &path); err == nil && path != "" {
   534→			return path
   535→		}
   536→	}
   537→	return ""
   538→}
   539→
   540→func extractLastAssistantResponse(entries []kiroHistoryEntry) string {
   541→	for i := len(entries) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   542→		if len(entries[i].Assistant) == 0 {
   543→			continue
   544→		}
   545→
   546→		var responseContent kiroResponseContent
   547→		if err := json.Unmarshal(entries[i].Assistant, &responseContent); err == nil && responseContent.Response.Content != "" {
   548→			return responseContent.Response.Content
   549→		}
   550→	}
   551→	return ""
   552→}
   553→
```

    77→	return protocol.AgentSessionJSON{

    28→	prodHookCommandBase = "entire hooks kiro "

    31→
    32→type ideHookDef struct {
    33→	Filename    string
    34→	TriggerType string
    35→	CLIVerb     string
    36→}
    37→
    38→var ideHookDefs = []ideHookDef{
    39→	{Filename: "entire-prompt-submit", TriggerType: "promptSubmit", CLIVerb: HookNameUserPromptSubmit},
    40→	{Filename: "entire-stop", TriggerType: "agentStop", CLIVerb: HookNameStop},
    41→	{Filename: "entire-pre-tool-use", TriggerType: "preToolUse", CLIVerb: HookNamePreToolUse},
    42→	{Filename: "entire-post-tool-use", TriggerType: "postToolUse", CLIVerb: HookNamePostToolUse},
    43→}
    44→
    45→func (a *Agent) ParseHook(hookName string, input []byte) (*protocol.EventJSON, error) {
    46→	var raw hookInputRaw
    47→	if len(input) > 0 {
    48→		if err := json.Unmarshal(input, &raw); err != nil {
    49→			return nil, err
    50→		}
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	switch hookName {
    54→	case HookNameAgentSpawn:
    55→		sessionID := a.generateAndCacheSessionID()
    56→		return &protocol.EventJSON{
    57→			Type:      1,
    58→			SessionID: sessionID,
    59→			Timestamp: time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    60→		}, nil
    61→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
    62→		sessionID := a.readCachedSessionID()
    63→		if sessionID == "" {
    64→			sessionID = a.generateAndCacheSessionID()
    65→		}
    66→		prompt := raw.Prompt
    67→		if prompt == "" {
    68→			prompt = os.Getenv("USER_PROMPT")
    69→		}
    70→		return &protocol.EventJSON{
    71→			Type:      2,
    72→			SessionID: sessionID,
    73→			Prompt:    prompt,
    74→			Timestamp: time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    75→		}, nil
    76→	case HookNamePreToolUse, HookNamePostToolUse:
    77→		return nil, nil
    78→	case HookNameStop:
    79→		cwd := raw.CWD
    80→		if cwd == "" {
    81→			cwd = protocol.RepoRoot()
    82→		}
    83→		sessionID := a.readCachedSessionID()
    84→		if sessionID == "" {
    85→			nativeSessionID, err := a.querySessionID(cwd)
    86→			if err == nil && nativeSessionID != "" {
    87→				sessionID = nativeSessionID
    88→			} else {
    89→				sessionID = fallbackStopSessionID()
    90→			}
    91→		}
    92→		sessionRef := a.captureTranscriptForStop(cwd, sessionID)
    93→		a.clearCachedSessionID()
    94→		return &protocol.EventJSON{
    95→			Type:       3,
    96→			SessionID:  sessionID,
    97→			SessionRef: sessionRef,
    98→			Timestamp:  time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    99→		}, nil
   100→	default:
   101→		return nil, nil
   102→	}
   103→}
   104→
   105→func (a *Agent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
   106→	repoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()
   107→	if !force && allHooksInstalled(repoRoot, localDev) && trustedCommandsPresent(repoRoot, localDev) {
   108→		return 0, nil
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	if err := writeCLIHooks(repoRoot, localDev); err != nil {
   112→		return 0, err
   113→	}
   114→	if err := writeIDEHooks(repoRoot, localDev); err != nil {
   115→		return 0, err
   116→	}
   117→	if err := installTrustedCommands(repoRoot, localDev); err != nil {
   118→		return 0, err
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	return len(defaultHookNames()) + len(ideHookDefs), nil
   122→}
   123→
   124→func (a *Agent) UninstallHooks() error {
   125→	repoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()
   126→	cliPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", hooksDir, hooksFileName)
   127→	if err := os.Remove(cliPath); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   128→		return err
   129→	}
   130→	for _, def := range ideHookDefs {
   131→		path := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", ideHooksDir, def.Filename+ideHookFileSuffix)
   132→		if err := os.Remove(path); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   133→			return err
   134→		}
   135→	}
   136→	if err := uninstallTrustedCommands(repoRoot); err != nil {
   137→		return err
   138→	}
   139→	return nil
   140→}
   141→
   142→func (a *Agent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   143→	repoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()
   144→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", "agents", "entire.json")); err == nil {
   145→		return true
   146→	}
   147→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", "hooks", "entire-stop.kiro.hook")); err == nil {
   148→		return true
   149→	}
   150→	return false
   151→}
   152→
   153→func defaultHookNames() []string {
   154→	return []string{
   155→		HookNameAgentSpawn,
   156→		HookNameUserPromptSubmit,
   157→		HookNamePreToolUse,
   158→		HookNamePostToolUse,
   159→		HookNameStop,
   160→	}
   161→}
   162→
   163→func writeCLIHooks(repoRoot string, localDev bool) error {
   164→	hooksPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", hooksDir, hooksFileName)
   165→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(hooksPath), 0o700); err != nil {
   166→		return err
   167→	}
   168→	commandBase := hookCommandBase(localDev)
   169→
   170→	file := kiroAgentFile{
   171→		Name: "entire",
   172→		Tools: []string{
   173→			"read", "write", "shell", "grep", "glob",
   174→			"aws", "report", "introspect", "knowledge",
   175→			"thinking", "todo", "delegate",
   176→		},
   177→		Hooks: kiroHooks{
   178→			AgentSpawn:       []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNameAgentSpawn}},
   179→			UserPromptSubmit: []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNameUserPromptSubmit}},
   180→			PreToolUse:       []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNamePreToolUse}},
   181→			PostToolUse:      []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNamePostToolUse}},
   182→			Stop:             []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNameStop}},
   183→		},
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	data, err := marshalJSON(file)
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		return err
   189→	}
   190→	return os.WriteFile(hooksPath, data, 0o600)
   191→}
   192→
   193→func writeIDEHooks(repoRoot string, localDev bool) error {
   194→	dir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", ideHooksDir)
   195→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
   196→		return err
   197→	}
   198→	commandBase := hookCommandBase(localDev)
   199→
   200→	for _, def := range ideHookDefs {
   201→		hook := kiroIDEHookFile{
   202→			Enabled:     true,
   203→			Name:        def.Filename,
   204→			Description: "Entire CLI " + def.TriggerType + " hook",
   205→			Version:     ideHookVersion,
   206→			When: kiroIDEHookWhen{
   207→				Type: def.TriggerType,
   208→			},
   209→			Then: kiroIDEHookThen{
   210→				Type:    "runCommand",
   211→				Command: shellWrappedCommand(commandBase + def.CLIVerb),
   212→			},
   213→		}
   214→		data, err := marshalJSON(hook)
   215→		if err != nil {
   216→			return err
   217→		}
   218→		path := filepath.Join(dir, def.Filename+ideHookFileSuffix)
   219→		if err := os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   220→			return err
   221→		}
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	return nil
   225→}
   226→
   227→func allHooksInstalled(repoRoot string, localDev bool) bool {
   228→	cliPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", hooksDir, hooksFileName)
   229→	commandBase := hookCommandBase(localDev)
   230→	if data, err := os.ReadFile(cliPath); err == nil {
   231→		var file kiroAgentFile
   232→		if json.Unmarshal(data, &file) == nil &&
   233→			hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.AgentSpawn, commandBase+HookNameAgentSpawn) &&
   234→			hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit, commandBase+HookNameUserPromptSubmit) &&
   235→			hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.PreToolUse, commandBase+HookNamePreToolUse) &&
   236→			hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.PostToolUse, commandBase+HookNamePostToolUse) &&
   237→			hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.Stop, commandBase+HookNameStop) &&
   238→			allIDEHooksPresent(repoRoot, localDev) {
   239→			return true
   240→		}
   241→	}
   242→	return false
   243→}
   244→
   245→func hookCommandExists(entries []kiroHookEntry, command string) bool {
   246→	for _, entry := range entries {
   247→		if entry.Command == command {
   248→			return true
   249→		}
   250→	}
   251→	return false
   252→}
   253→
   254→func allIDEHooksPresent(repoRoot string, localDev bool) bool {
   255→	commandBase := hookCommandBase(localDev)
   256→	for _, def := range ideHookDefs {
   257→		path := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", ideHooksDir, def.Filename+ideHookFileSuffix)
   258→		data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
   259→		if err != nil {
   260→			return false
   261→		}
   262→		var hook kiroIDEHookFile
   263→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &hook); err != nil {
   264→			return false
   265→		}
   266→		if hook.Then.Command != shellWrappedCommand(commandBase+def.CLIVerb) {
   267→			return false
   268→		}
   269→	}
   270→	return true
   271→}
   272→
   273→func trustedCommandsPresent(repoRoot string, localDev bool) bool {
   274→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, vscodeSettingsDir, vscodeSettingsFile)
   275→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return false
   278→	}
   279→	var settings map[string]json.RawMessage
   280→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   281→		return false
   282→	}
   283→	raw, ok := settings[trustedCommandsKey]
   284→	if !ok {
   285→		return false
   286→	}
   287→	var commands []string
   288→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &commands); err != nil {
   289→		return false
   290→	}
   291→	want := trustedCommand(localDev)
   292→	for _, command := range commands {
   293→		if command == want {
   294→			return true
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→	return false
   298→}
   299→
   300→func installTrustedCommands(repoRoot string, localDev bool) error {
   301→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, vscodeSettingsDir, vscodeSettingsFile)
   302→
   303→	settings, err := readSettings(settingsPath)
   304→	if err != nil {
   305→		return err
   306→	}
   307→	commands, err := readTrustedCommands(settings)
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		return err
   310→	}
   311→	want := trustedCommand(localDev)
   312→	for _, command := range commands {
   313→		if command == want {
   314→			return nil
   315→		}
   316→	}
   317→	commands = append(commands, want)
   318→	raw, err := json.Marshal(commands)
   319→	if err != nil {
   320→		return err
   321→	}
   322→	settings[trustedCommandsKey] = raw
   323→	return writeSettings(settingsPath, settings)
   324→}
   325→
   326→func uninstallTrustedCommands(repoRoot string) error {
   327→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, vscodeSettingsDir, vscodeSettingsFile)
   328→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err != nil {
   329→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   330→			return nil
   331→		}
   332→		return err
   333→	}
   334→	settings, err := readSettings(settingsPath)
   335→	if err != nil {
   336→		return err
   337→	}
   338→	commands, err := readTrustedCommands(settings)
   339→	if err != nil {
   340→		return err
   341→	}
   342→	filtered := commands[:0]
   343→	for _, command := range commands {
   344→		if command != prodTrustedCommand && command != localDevTrustedCmd {
   345→			filtered = append(filtered, command)
   346→		}
   347→	}
   348→	if len(filtered) == 0 {
   349→		delete(settings, trustedCommandsKey)
   350→	} else {
   351→		raw, err := json.Marshal(filtered)
   352→		if err != nil {
   353→			return err
   354→		}
   355→		settings[trustedCommandsKey] = raw
   356→	}
   357→	return writeSettings(settingsPath, settings)
   358→}
   359→
   360→func hookCommandBase(localDev bool) string {
   361→	if localDev {
   362→		return localDevCommandBase
   363→	}
   364→	return prodHookCommandBase
   365→}
   366→
   367→// shellWrappedCommand wraps a hook command in "sh -c" with a /dev/null stdin
   368→// redirect. IDEs typically run hook commands directly without a shell, so a
   369→// bare "</dev/null" suffix is passed as a literal argument instead of being
   370→// interpreted as a redirect. Wrapping in sh ensures the redirect works.
   371→// The command content is built from compile-time constants (not user input).
   372→func shellWrappedCommand(cmd string) string {
   373→	return "sh -c '" + cmd + " </dev/null'"
   374→}
   375→
   376→func trustedCommand(localDev bool) string {
   377→	if localDev {
   378→		return localDevTrustedCmd
   379→	}
   380→	return prodTrustedCommand
   381→}
   382→
   383→func readSettings(path string) (map[string]json.RawMessage, error) {
   384→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
   385→	if err != nil {
   386→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   387→			return map[string]json.RawMessage{}, nil
   388→		}
   389→		return nil, err
   390→	}
   391→	var settings map[string]json.RawMessage
   392→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   393→		return nil, err
   394→	}
   395→	if settings == nil {
   396→		settings = map[string]json.RawMessage{}
   397→	}
   398→	return settings, nil
   399→}
   400→
   401→func readTrustedCommands(settings map[string]json.RawMessage) ([]string, error) {
   402→	raw, ok := settings[trustedCommandsKey]
   403→	if !ok {
   404→		return []string{}, nil
   405→	}
   406→	var commands []string
   407→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &commands); err != nil {
   408→		return nil, err
   409→	}
   410→	return commands, nil
   411→}
   412→
   413→func writeSettings(path string, settings map[string]json.RawMessage) error {
   414→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o700); err != nil {
   415→		return err
   416→	}
   417→	data, err := marshalJSON(settings)
   418→	if err != nil {
   419→		return err
   420→	}
   421→	return os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o600)
   422→}
   423→
   424→func marshalJSON(v any) ([]byte, error) {
   425→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(v, "", "  ")
   426→	if err != nil {
   427→		return nil, err
   428→	}
   429→	return append(data, '\n'), nil
   430→}
   431→
   432→func (a *Agent) generateAndCacheSessionID() string {
   433→	sessionID := generateSessionID()
   434→	cachePath := a.sessionIDCachePath()
   435→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(cachePath), 0o700); err == nil {
   436→		_ = os.WriteFile(cachePath, []byte(sessionID), 0o600)
   437→	}
   438→	return sessionID
   439→}
   440→
   441→func (a *Agent) readCachedSessionID() string {
   442→	data, err := os.ReadFile(a.sessionIDCachePath())
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		return ""
   445→	}
   446→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(data))
   447→}
   448→
   449→func (a *Agent) clearCachedSessionID() {
   450→	_ = os.Remove(a.sessionIDCachePath())
   451→}
   452→
   453→func (a *Agent) sessionIDCachePath() string {
   454→	return filepath.Join(protocol.RepoRoot(), ".entire", "tmp", sessionIDFile)
   455→}
   456→
   457→func fallbackStopSessionID() string {
   458→	return generateSessionID()
   459→}
   460→
   461→func generateSessionID() string {
   462→	buf := make([]byte, 16)
   463→	if _, err := rand.Read(buf); err != nil {
   464→		return fmt.Sprintf("kiro-%d", time.Now().UnixNano())
   465→	}
   466→	return hex.EncodeToString(buf)
   467→}
   468→

     1→package protocol
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"flag"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→	"time"
    11→)
    12→
    13→type sessionDirResolver interface {
    14→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    15→}
    16→
    17→type sessionFileResolver interface {
    18→	ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, sessionID string) string
    19→}
    20→
    21→type sessionIDProvider interface {
    22→	GetSessionID(*HookInputJSON) string
    23→}
    24→
    25→type sessionReader interface {
    26→	ReadSession(*HookInputJSON) (AgentSessionJSON, error)
    27→}
    28→
    29→type sessionWriter interface {
    30→	WriteSession(AgentSessionJSON) error
    31→}
    32→
    33→type transcriptReader interface {
    34→	ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    35→}

    38→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    39→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    40→}
    41→
    42→type resumeFormatter interface {
    43→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    44→}
    45→
    46→type hookParser interface {
    47→	ParseHook(hookName string, input []byte) (*EventJSON, error)
    48→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
    49→	UninstallHooks() error
    50→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
    51→}
    52→
    53→type transcriptAnalyzer interface {
    54→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
    55→	ExtractModifiedFiles(path string, offset int) ([]string, int, error)
    56→	ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, offset int) ([]string, error)
    57→	ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, bool, error)
    58→}
    59→
    60→func WriteJSON(w io.Writer, v any) error {
    61→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
    62→	enc.SetEscapeHTML(false)
    63→	return enc.Encode(v)
    64→}
    65→
    66→func ReadJSON[T any](r io.Reader) (*T, error) {
    67→	var value T
    68→	if err := json.NewDecoder(r).Decode(&value); err != nil {
    69→		return nil, err
    70→	}
    71→	return &value, nil
    72→}
    73→
    74→func RepoRoot() string {
    75→	if root := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_REPO_ROOT"); root != "" {
    76→		return root
    77→	}
    78→	root, _ := os.Getwd()
    79→	return root
    80→}
    81→
    82→func HandleGetSessionID(stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, provider sessionIDProvider) error {
    83→	input, err := ReadJSON[HookInputJSON](stdin)
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		return err
    86→	}
    87→	return WriteJSON(stdout, SessionIDResponse{SessionID: provider.GetSessionID(input)})
    88→}
    89→
    90→func HandleGetSessionDir(args []string, stdout io.Writer, resolver sessionDirResolver) error {
    91→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("get-session-dir", flag.ContinueOnError)
    92→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
    93→	repoPath := fs.String("repo-path", "", "repo path")
    94→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
    95→		return err
    96→	}
    97→	sessionDir, err := resolver.GetSessionDir(*repoPath)
    98→	if err != nil {
    99→		return err
   100→	}
   101→	return WriteJSON(stdout, SessionDirResponse{SessionDir: sessionDir})
   102→}
   103→
   104→func HandleResolveSessionFile(args []string, stdout io.Writer, resolver sessionFileResolver) error {
   105→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("resolve-session-file", flag.ContinueOnError)
   106→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   107→	sessionDir := fs.String("session-dir", "", "session dir")
   108→	sessionID := fs.String("session-id", "", "session id")
   109→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   110→		return err
   111→	}
   112→	return WriteJSON(stdout, SessionFileResponse{SessionFile: resolver.ResolveSessionFile(*sessionDir, *sessionID)})
   113→}
   114→
   115→func HandleReadSession(stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, reader sessionReader) error {
   116→	input, err := ReadJSON[HookInputJSON](stdin)
   117→	if err != nil {
   118→		return err
   119→	}
   120→	session, err := reader.ReadSession(input)
   121→	if err != nil {
   122→		return err
   123→	}
   124→	return WriteJSON(stdout, session)
   125→}
   126→
   127→func HandleWriteSession(stdin io.Reader, writer sessionWriter) error {
   128→	session, err := ReadJSON[AgentSessionJSON](stdin)
   129→	if err != nil {
   130→		return err
   131→	}
   132→	return writer.WriteSession(*session)
   133→}
   134→
   135→func HandleReadTranscript(args []string, stdout io.Writer, reader transcriptReader) error {
   136→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("read-transcript", flag.ContinueOnError)
   137→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   138→	sessionRef := fs.String("session-ref", "", "session ref")
   139→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   140→		return err
   141→	}
   142→	data, err := reader.ReadTranscript(*sessionRef)
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		return err
   145→	}
   146→	_, err = stdout.Write(data)
   147→	return err
   148→}
   149→
   150→func HandleChunkTranscript(args []string, stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, chunker transcriptChunker) error {
   151→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("chunk-transcript", flag.ContinueOnError)
   152→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   153→	maxSize := fs.Int("max-size", 0, "max size")
   154→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   155→		return err
   156→	}
   157→	content, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return err
   160→	}
   161→	chunks, err := chunker.ChunkTranscript(content, *maxSize)
   162→	if err != nil {
   163→		return err
   164→	}
   165→	return WriteJSON(stdout, ChunkResponse{Chunks: chunks})
   166→}
   167→
   168→func HandleReassembleTranscript(stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, chunker transcriptChunker) error {
   169→	input, err := ReadJSON[ChunkResponse](stdin)
   170→	if err != nil {
   171→		return err
   172→	}
   173→	data, err := chunker.ReassembleTranscript(input.Chunks)
   174→	if err != nil {
   175→		return err
   176→	}
   177→	_, err = stdout.Write(data)
   178→	return err
   179→}
   180→
   181→func HandleFormatResumeCommand(args []string, stdout io.Writer, formatter resumeFormatter) error {
   182→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("format-resume-command", flag.ContinueOnError)
   183→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   184→	sessionID := fs.String("session-id", "", "session id")
   185→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   186→		return err
   187→	}
   188→	return WriteJSON(stdout, ResumeCommandResponse{Command: formatter.FormatResumeCommand(*sessionID)})
   189→}
   190→
   191→// readStdinWithTimeout reads from r but returns empty data if nothing arrives
   192→// within the timeout. This prevents blocking when an IDE keeps stdin open
   193→// without sending data (e.g., for hooks that have no payload).
   194→func readStdinWithTimeout(r io.Reader, timeout time.Duration) ([]byte, error) {
   195→	type result struct {
   196→		data []byte
   197→		err  error
   198→	}
   199→	ch := make(chan result, 1)
   200→	go func() {
   201→		data, err := io.ReadAll(r)
   202→		ch <- result{data, err}
   203→	}()
   204→	select {
   205→	case res := <-ch:
   206→		return res.data, res.err
   207→	case <-time.After(timeout):
   208→		return nil, nil
   209→	}
   210→}
   211→
   212→func HandleParseHook(args []string, stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, parser hookParser) error {
   213→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("parse-hook", flag.ContinueOnError)
   214→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   215→	hook := fs.String("hook", "", "hook name")
   216→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   217→		return err
   218→	}
   219→	input, err := readStdinWithTimeout(stdin, 100*time.Millisecond)
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return err
   222→	}
   223→	event, err := parser.ParseHook(*hook, input)
   224→	if err != nil {
   225→		return err
   226→	}
   227→	if event == nil {
   228→		_, err = io.WriteString(stdout, "null\n")
   229→		return err
   230→	}
   231→	return WriteJSON(stdout, event)
   232→}
   233→
   234→func HandleInstallHooks(args []string, stdout io.Writer, parser hookParser) error {
   235→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("install-hooks", flag.ContinueOnError)
   236→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   237→	localDev := fs.Bool("local-dev", false, "local dev")
   238→	force := fs.Bool("force", false, "force")
   239→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   240→		return err
   241→	}
   242→	count, err := parser.InstallHooks(*localDev, *force)
   243→	if err != nil {
   244→		return err
   245→	}
   246→	return WriteJSON(stdout, HooksInstalledCountResponse{HooksInstalled: count})
   247→}
   248→
   249→func HandleGetTranscriptPosition(args []string, stdout io.Writer, analyzer transcriptAnalyzer) error {
   250→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("get-transcript-position", flag.ContinueOnError)
   251→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   252→	path := fs.String("path", "", "path")
   253→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   254→		return err
   255→	}
   256→	position, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(*path)
   257→	if err != nil {
   258→		return err
   259→	}
   260→	return WriteJSON(stdout, TranscriptPositionResponse{Position: position})
   261→}
   262→
   263→func HandleExtractModifiedFiles(args []string, stdout io.Writer, analyzer transcriptAnalyzer) error {
   264→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("extract-modified-files", flag.ContinueOnError)
   265→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   266→	path := fs.String("path", "", "path")
   267→	offset := fs.Int("offset", 0, "offset")
   268→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   269→		return err
   270→	}
   271→	files, currentPosition, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFiles(*path, *offset)
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		return err
   274→	}
   275→	return WriteJSON(stdout, ExtractFilesResponse{Files: files, CurrentPosition: currentPosition})
   276→}
   277→
   278→func HandleExtractPrompts(args []string, stdout io.Writer, analyzer transcriptAnalyzer) error {
   279→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("extract-prompts", flag.ContinueOnError)
   280→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   281→	sessionRef := fs.String("session-ref", "", "session ref")
   282→	offset := fs.Int("offset", 0, "offset")
   283→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   284→		return err
   285→	}
   286→	prompts, err := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(*sessionRef, *offset)
   287→	if err != nil {
   288→		return err
   289→	}
   290→	return WriteJSON(stdout, ExtractPromptsResponse{Prompts: prompts})
   291→}
   292→
   293→func HandleExtractSummary(args []string, stdout io.Writer, analyzer transcriptAnalyzer) error {
   294→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("extract-summary", flag.ContinueOnError)
   295→	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
   296→	sessionRef := fs.String("session-ref", "", "session ref")
   297→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
   298→		return err
   299→	}
   300→	summary, hasSummary, err := analyzer.ExtractSummary(*sessionRef)
   301→	if err != nil {
   302→		return err
   303→	}
   304→	return WriteJSON(stdout, ExtractSummaryResponse{Summary: summary, HasSummary: hasSummary})
   305→}
   306→
   307→func DefaultSessionDir(repoPath string) string {
   308→	return filepath.Join(repoPath, ".entire", "tmp")
   309→}
   310→
   311→func ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, sessionID string) string {
   312→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, fmt.Sprintf("%s.json", sessionID))
   313→}

    19→// RepoState holds the working state for a single test's cloned repository.
    20→type RepoState struct {
    21→	Agent            agents.Agent
    22→	Dir              string
    23→	ArtifactDir      string
    24→	HeadBefore       string
    25→	CheckpointBefore string
    26→	ConsoleLog       *os.File
    27→	session          agents.Session // interactive session, if started via StartSession
    28→	skipArtifacts    bool           // suppresses artifact capture on scenario restart
    29→}
    30→
    31→// SetupRepo creates a fresh git repository in a temporary directory, seeds it
    32→// with an initial commit, and runs `entire enable` for the given agent.
    33→// Artifact capture is registered as a cleanup function.
    34→func SetupRepo(t *testing.T, agent agents.Agent) *RepoState {
    35→	t.Helper()
    36→
    37→	keepRepos := os.Getenv("E2E_KEEP_REPOS") != ""
    38→
    39→	dir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "e2e-repo-*")
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		t.Fatalf("create temp dir: %v", err)
    42→	}
    43→	if keepRepos {
    44→		t.Logf("E2E_KEEP_REPOS: repo will be preserved at %s", dir)
    45→	} else {
    46→		t.Cleanup(func() { os.RemoveAll(dir) })
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	// Resolve symlinks (macOS: /var -> /private/var) so paths match
    50→	// what agent CLIs see when they resolve their own CWD.
    51→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(dir); err == nil {
    52→		dir = resolved
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	Git(t, dir, "init")
    56→	Git(t, dir, "config", "user.name", "E2E Test")
    57→	Git(t, dir, "config", "user.email", "e2e@test.local")
    58→	Git(t, dir, "commit", "--allow-empty", "-m", "initial commit")
    59→
    60→	// External agents need external_agents enabled in settings before enable.
    61→	if ea, ok := agent.(agents.ExternalAgent); ok && ea.IsExternalAgent() {
    62→		entireDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".entire")
    63→		if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
    64→			t.Fatalf("create .entire for external agent: %v", err)
    65→		}
    66→		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(entireDir, "settings.json"),
    67→			[]byte("{\"external_agents\": true}\n"), 0o644); err != nil {
    68→			t.Fatalf("write external_agents setting: %v", err)
    69→		}
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	entire.Enable(t, dir, agent.EntireAgent())
    73→	PatchSettings(t, dir, map[string]any{"log_level": "debug"})
    74→
    75→	// Create artifact dir eagerly so console.log is written to disk
    76→	// incrementally. Even if the test is killed by a global timeout,
    77→	// partial output survives.
    78→	artDir := artifactDir(t)
    79→	consoleLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(artDir, "console.log"))
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		t.Fatalf("create console.log: %v", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	state := &RepoState{
    85→		Agent:            agent,
    86→		Dir:              dir,
    87→		ArtifactDir:      artDir,
    88→		HeadBefore:       GitOutput(t, dir, "rev-parse", "HEAD"),
    89→		CheckpointBefore: GitOutput(t, dir, "rev-parse", "entire/checkpoints/v1"),
    90→		ConsoleLog:       consoleLog,
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	t.Cleanup(func() {
    94→		_ = consoleLog.Close()
    95→		if !state.skipArtifacts {
    96→			CaptureArtifacts(t, state)
    97→		}
    98→	})
    99→
   100→	return state
   101→}
   102→
   103→// ForEachAgent runs fn as a parallel subtest for every registered agent.
   104→// It handles repo setup, concurrency gating, context timeout, and cleanup.
   105→// The timeout is scaled by each agent's TimeoutMultiplier.
   106→func ForEachAgent(t *testing.T, timeout time.Duration, fn func(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, ctx context.Context)) {
   107→	t.Helper()
   108→	t.Parallel()
   109→	all := agents.All()
   110→	if len(all) == 0 {
   111→		t.Skip("no agents registered (check E2E_AGENT filter)")
   112→	}
   113→	for _, agent := range all {
   114→		t.Run(agent.Name(), func(t *testing.T) {
   115→			t.Parallel()
   116→			slotCtx := context.Background()
   117→			if deadline, ok := t.Deadline(); ok {
   118→				var cancel context.CancelFunc
   119→				slotCtx, cancel = context.WithDeadline(slotCtx, deadline)
   120→				defer cancel()
   121→			}
   122→			if err := agents.AcquireSlot(slotCtx, agent); err != nil {
   123→				t.Fatalf("timed out waiting for agent slot: %v", err)
   124→			}
   125→			defer agents.ReleaseSlot(agent)
   126→
   127→			scaled := time.Duration(float64(timeout) * agent.TimeoutMultiplier())
   128→
   129→			var prevState *RepoState
   130→			for attempt := range maxScenarioRestarts + 1 {
   131→				s := SetupRepo(t, agent)
   132→				ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), scaled)
   133→
   134→				if prevState != nil {
   135→					prevState.skipArtifacts = true
   136→				}
   137→
   138→				restarted := runScenario(t, s, ctx, fn)
   139→				cancel()
   140→
   141→				if !restarted {
   142→					return
   143→				}
   144→				prevState = s
   145→				if attempt >= maxScenarioRestarts {
   146→					t.Fatalf("exhausted %d scenario attempts due to transient API errors", maxScenarioRestarts+1)
   147→				}
   148→				t.Logf("transient error, restarting scenario (attempt %d/%d)", attempt+2, maxScenarioRestarts+1)
   149→			}
   150→		})
   151→	}
   152→}
   153→
   154→func runScenario(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, ctx context.Context, fn func(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, ctx context.Context)) (restarted bool) {
   155→	t.Helper()
   156→	defer func() {
   157→		if r := recover(); r != nil {
   158→			if _, ok := r.(errScenarioRestart); ok {
   159→				restarted = true
   160→				return
   161→			}
   162→			panic(r)
   163→		}
   164→	}()
   165→	fn(t, s, ctx)
   166→	return false
   167→}
   168→
   169→const maxScenarioRestarts = 2
   170→
   171→type errScenarioRestart struct {
   172→	msg string
   173→}
   174→
   175→// RunPrompt runs an agent prompt, logs the command and output to ConsoleLog,
   176→// and returns the result. If the agent reports a transient API error, it
   177→// panics with errScenarioRestart to trigger a full scenario restart.
   178→func (s *RepoState) RunPrompt(t *testing.T, ctx context.Context, prompt string, opts ...agents.Option) (agents.Output, error) {
   179→	t.Helper()
   180→	out, err := s.Agent.RunPrompt(ctx, s.Dir, prompt, opts...)
   181→	s.logPromptResult(out)
   182→
   183→	if err != nil && s.Agent.IsTransientError(out, err) {
   184→		errMsg := fmt.Sprintf("transient API error (stderr: %s)", strings.TrimSpace(out.Stderr))
   185→		t.Logf("%s — restarting scenario", errMsg)
   186→		fmt.Fprintf(s.ConsoleLog, "> [transient] %s — restarting scenario\n", errMsg)
   187→		panic(errScenarioRestart{msg: errMsg})
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	return out, err
   191→}
   192→
   193→func (s *RepoState) logPromptResult(out agents.Output) {
   194→	s.ConsoleLog.WriteString("> " + out.Command + "\n")
   195→	s.ConsoleLog.WriteString("stdout:\n" + out.Stdout + "\n")
   196→	s.ConsoleLog.WriteString("stderr:\n" + out.Stderr + "\n")
   197→}
   198→
   199→// Git runs a git command in the repo and logs it to ConsoleLog.
   200→func (s *RepoState) Git(t *testing.T, args ...string) {
   201→	t.Helper()
   202→	s.ConsoleLog.WriteString("> git " + strings.Join(args, " ") + "\n")
   203→	Git(t, s.Dir, args...)
   204→}
   205→
   206→// GitOutput runs a git command and returns its trimmed stdout.
   207→func (s *RepoState) GitOutput(t *testing.T, args ...string) string {
   208→	t.Helper()
   209→	return GitOutput(t, s.Dir, args...)
   210→}
   211→
   212→// StartSession starts an interactive session and registers it for pane
   213→// capture in artifacts. Returns nil if the agent does not support interactive
   214→// mode. The session is closed automatically during test cleanup.
   215→func (s *RepoState) StartSession(t *testing.T, ctx context.Context) agents.Session {
   216→	t.Helper()
   217→	session, err := s.Agent.StartSession(ctx, s.Dir)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		t.Fatalf("start session: %v", err)
   220→	}
   221→	if session == nil {
   222→		return nil
   223→	}
   224→	s.session = session
   225→	return session
   226→}
   227→
   228→// WaitFor waits for a pattern in the interactive session's pane and logs the
   229→// pane content to ConsoleLog after the wait completes.
   230→func (s *RepoState) WaitFor(t *testing.T, session agents.Session, pattern string, timeout time.Duration) {
   231→	t.Helper()
   232→	content, err := session.WaitFor(pattern, timeout)
   233→	fmt.Fprintf(s.ConsoleLog, "> pane after WaitFor(%q):\n%s\n", pattern, content)
   234→	if err != nil {
   235→		t.Fatalf("WaitFor(%q): %v", pattern, err)
   236→	}
   237→}
   238→
   239→// IsExternalAgent returns true if the agent implements the ExternalAgent
   240→// interface and reports itself as external.
   241→func (s *RepoState) IsExternalAgent() bool {
   242→	ea, ok := s.Agent.(agents.ExternalAgent)
   243→	return ok && ea.IsExternalAgent()
   244→}
   245→
   246→// Send sends input to an interactive session and logs it to ConsoleLog.
   247→func (s *RepoState) Send(t *testing.T, session agents.Session, input string) {
   248→	t.Helper()
   249→	s.ConsoleLog.WriteString("> send: " + input + "\n")
   250→	if err := session.Send(input); err != nil {
   251→		t.Fatalf("send failed: %v", err)
   252→	}
   253→}
   254→
   255→// PatchSettings merges extra keys into .entire/settings.json.
   256→func PatchSettings(t *testing.T, dir string, extra map[string]any) {
   257→	t.Helper()
   258→	path := filepath.Join(dir, ".entire", "settings.json")
   259→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		t.Fatalf("read settings: %v", err)
   262→	}
   263→	var settings map[string]any
   264→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   265→		t.Fatalf("parse settings: %v", err)
   266→	}
   267→	for k, v := range extra {
   268→		settings[k] = v
   269→	}
   270→	out, err := json.MarshalIndent(settings, "", "  ")
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		t.Fatalf("marshal settings: %v", err)
   273→	}
   274→	if err := os.WriteFile(path, out, 0o644); err != nil {
   275→		t.Fatalf("write settings: %v", err)
   276→	}
   277→}
   278→
   279→// Git runs a git command in the given directory and fails the test on error.
   280→func Git(t *testing.T, dir string, args ...string) {
   281→	t.Helper()
   282→
   283→	cmd := exec.Command("git", args...)
   284→	if dir != "" {
   285→		cmd.Dir = dir
   286→	}
   287→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0")
   288→
   289→	out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		t.Fatalf("git %s failed: %v\n%s", strings.Join(args, " "), err, out)
   292→	}
   293→}
   294→
   295→// GitOutput runs a git command in the given directory, returns its trimmed
   296→// stdout, and fails the test on error.
   297→func GitOutput(t *testing.T, dir string, args ...string) string {
   298→	t.Helper()
   299→
   300→	cmd := exec.Command("git", args...)
   301→	if dir != "" {
   302→		cmd.Dir = dir
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   306→	if err != nil {
   307→		var stderr string
   308→		ee := &exec.ExitError{}
   309→		if errors.As(err, &ee) {
   310→			stderr = string(ee.Stderr)
   311→		}
   312→		t.Fatalf("git %s failed: %v\n%s", strings.Join(args, " "), err, stderr)
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   316→}
   317→
   318→// NewCheckpointCommits returns the SHAs of commits added to the
   319→// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch since the test was set up, oldest first.
   320→func NewCheckpointCommits(t *testing.T, s *RepoState) []string {
   321→	t.Helper()
   322→
   323→	log := GitOutput(t, s.Dir, "log", "--reverse", "--format=%H", s.CheckpointBefore+"..entire/checkpoints/v1")
   324→	if log == "" {
   325→		return nil
   326→	}
   327→	return strings.Split(log, "\n")
   328→}
   329→
   330→// CheckpointIDs lists all checkpoint IDs from the tree at the tip of the
   331→// checkpoint branch.
   332→func CheckpointIDs(t *testing.T, dir string) []string {
   333→	t.Helper()
   334→	out := gitOutputSafe(dir, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "entire/checkpoints/v1")
   335→	if out == "" {
   336→		return nil
   337→	}
   338→	seen := map[string]bool{}
   339→	var ids []string
   340→	for _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(out), "\n") {
   341→		parts := strings.Split(line, "/")
   342→		if len(parts) == 3 && parts[2] == "metadata.json" {
   343→			id := parts[0] + parts[1]
   344→			if !seen[id] {
   345→				seen[id] = true
   346→				ids = append(ids, id)
   347→			}
   348→		}
   349→	}
   350→	return ids
   351→}
   352→
   353→// ReadCheckpointMetadata reads checkpoint-level metadata.json from the
   354→// checkpoint branch for the given checkpoint ID.
   355→func ReadCheckpointMetadata(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string) CheckpointMetadata {
   356→	t.Helper()
   357→
   358→	path := CheckpointPath(checkpointID) + "/metadata.json"
   359→	blob := "entire/checkpoints/v1:" + path
   360→
   361→	raw := GitOutput(t, dir, "show", blob)
   362→
   363→	var meta CheckpointMetadata
   364→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &meta); err != nil {
   365→		t.Fatalf("unmarshal checkpoint metadata from %s: %v", blob, err)
   366→	}
   367→
   368→	return meta
   369→}
   370→
   371→// ReadSessionMetadata reads a session's metadata.json from the checkpoint
   372→// branch for the given checkpoint ID and session index.
   373→func ReadSessionMetadata(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string, sessionIndex int) SessionMetadata {
   374→	t.Helper()
   375→
   376→	path := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d/metadata.json", CheckpointPath(checkpointID), sessionIndex)
   377→	blob := "entire/checkpoints/v1:" + path
   378→
   379→	raw := GitOutput(t, dir, "show", blob)
   380→
   381→	var meta SessionMetadata
   382→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &meta); err != nil {
   383→		t.Fatalf("unmarshal session metadata from %s: %v", blob, err)
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	return meta
   387→}
   388→
   389→// SetupBareRemote creates a bare git repo, adds it as "origin", and pushes
   390→// the initial commit. Returns the bare repo path.
   391→func SetupBareRemote(t *testing.T, s *RepoState) string {
   392→	t.Helper()
   393→
   394→	var bareDir string
   395→	if os.Getenv("E2E_KEEP_REPOS") != "" {
   396→		var err error
   397→		bareDir, err = os.MkdirTemp("", "e2e-bare-*")
   398→		if err != nil {
   399→			t.Fatalf("create bare remote dir: %v", err)
   400→		}
   401→		t.Logf("E2E_KEEP_REPOS: bare remote will be preserved at %s", bareDir)
   402→	} else {
   403→		bareDir = t.TempDir()
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	Git(t, bareDir, "init", "--bare")
   407→	Git(t, s.Dir, "remote", "add", "origin", bareDir)
   408→	Git(t, s.Dir, "push", "-u", "origin", "HEAD")
   409→	return bareDir
   410→}
   411→
   412→// GitOutputErr runs a git command and returns (output, error) without
   413→// failing the test.
   414→func GitOutputErr(dir string, args ...string) (string, error) {
   415→	cmd := exec.Command("git", args...)
   416→	if dir != "" {
   417→		cmd.Dir = dir
   418→	}
   419→	out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   420→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), err
   421→}
   422→
   423→// GetCheckpointTrailer extracts the Entire-Checkpoint trailer value from a
   424→// code commit. Returns the trimmed trailer value, or an empty string if the
   425→// trailer is not present.
   426→func GetCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, dir string, ref string) string {
   427→	t.Helper()
   428→
   429→	return GitOutput(t, dir, "log", "-1", "--format=%(trailers:key=Entire-Checkpoint,valueonly)", ref)
   430→}
   431→

    15→func TestKiro_Info(t *testing.T) {
    16→	t.Parallel()
    17→	env := NewKiroTestEnv(t)
    18→
    19→	var resp struct {
    20→		ProtocolVersion int      `json:"protocol_version"`
    21→		Name            string   `json:"name"`
    22→		Type            string   `json:"type"`
    23→		Description     string   `json:"description"`
    24→		IsPreview       bool     `json:"is_preview"`
    25→		ProtectedDirs   []string `json:"protected_dirs"`
    26→		HookNames       []string `json:"hook_names"`
    27→		Capabilities    struct {
    28→			Hooks              bool `json:"hooks"`
    29→			TranscriptAnalyzer bool `json:"transcript_analyzer"`
    30→		} `json:"capabilities"`
    31→	}
    32→	env.Runner.RunJSON(t, &resp, "", "info")
    33→
    34→	if resp.ProtocolVersion != 1 {
    35→		t.Errorf("protocol_version = %d, want 1", resp.ProtocolVersion)
    36→	}
    37→	if resp.Name != "kiro" {

    40→	if resp.Type != "Kiro" {

   170→
   171→	// Verify the file was written

   198→func TestKiro_ReadTranscript(t *testing.T) {

   201→
   202→	transcript := NewKiroTranscript("conv-1").AddPrompt("hello").AddResponse("summarize", "done")
   203→	transcriptPath := transcript.WriteToFile(t, env, "transcript.json")
   204→
   205→	result := env.Runner.MustSucceed(t, "", "read-transcript", "-session-ref", transcriptPath)
   206→	if len(result.Stdout) == 0 {
   207→		t.Error("read-transcript returned empty stdout")
